# README.md
 47 | 
 48 | ## 🗄️ Optional: Enable History Tracking with IBM Cloudant
 49 | 
 50 | LegacyLink AI can save your modernization history to IBM Cloudant NoSQL database for tracking and analytics.
 51 | 
 52 | > **Note**: This feature is **completely optional**. The application works perfectly without Cloudant configured.
 53 | 
----
 56 | 1. **Create IBM Cloud Account** - Visit [cloud.ibm.com](https://cloud.ibm.com)
 57 | 2. **Create Cloudant Service** - Use the free Lite plan
 58 | 3. **Configure Environment Variables**:
----
 60 |    cp .env.example .env
 61 |    # Edit .env with your Cloudant credentials
 62 |    ```
----

1503 | # IBM Cloudant NoSQL Integration Plan for LegacyLink AI
1504 | 
----
1506 | 
1507 | This document outlines the step-by-step plan to integrate IBM Cloudant NoSQL database to save and retrieve users' modernization history in the LegacyLink AI project.
1508 | 
1509 | ## Why IBM Cloudant?
1510 | 
1511 | IBM Cloudant is a fully managed, distributed NoSQL database optimized for heavy workloads and fast-growing web and mobile applications. It's ideal for this project because:
1512 | 
----
1527 |     C --> D[Create ZIP package]
1528 |     D --> E[Save to Cloudant]
1529 |     E --> F[Return ZIP to user]
1530 | 
1531 |     G[User requests history] --> H[Query Cloudant]
1532 |     H --> I[Display history in UI]
1533 | 
1534 |     E --> J[Cloudant Database]
1535 |     H --> J
----
1541 | 
1542 | ### Document Structure in Cloudant
1543 | 
----
1618 | 
1619 | ### Step 1: Set Up IBM Cloudant Account
1620 | 
----
1622 | - IBM Cloud account
1623 | - Cloudant service instance
1624 | 
----

1627 | 2. Create a Cloudant service instance
1628 | 3. Generate service credentials with appropriate permissions
1629 | 4. Note down the following credentials:
1630 |    - `CLOUDANT_URL`
1631 |    - `CLOUDANT_APIKEY`
1632 |    - `CLOUDANT_DATABASE_NAME`
1633 |

<file><path>storage/cloudant_client.py</path>
<content lines="1-260">
  1 | """
  2 | IBM Cloudant NoSQL client for LegacyLink AI
  3 | Handles saving and retrieving modernization history
  4 | """
  5 | from typing import Dict, List, Optional
  6 | from datetime import datetime, timezone
  7 | import uuid
  8 | from ibmcloudant.cloudant_v1 import CloudantV1, Document
  9 | from ibm_cloud_sdk_core.authenticators import IAMAuthenticator
 10 | from config.cloudant_config import CloudantConfig
 11 | 
 12 | 
 13 | class CloudantClient:
 14 |     """Client for IBM Cloudant NoSQL database operations"""
 15 |     
 16 |     def __init__(self, config: CloudantConfig):
 17 |         """
 18 |         Initialize Cloudant client
 19 |         
 20 |         Args:
 21 |             config: CloudantConfig instance with connection details
 22 |         """
 23 |         self.config = config
 24 |         self.client = None
 25 |         self.database_name = config.database_name
 26 |         
 27 |         if config.is_configured():
 28 |             self._initialize_client()
 29 |     
 30 |     def _initialize_client(self):
 31 |         """Initialize the Cloudant client with authentication"""
 32 |         try:
 33 |             authenticator = IAMAuthenticator(self.config.apikey)
 34 |             self.client = CloudantV1(authenticator=authenticator)
 35 |             self.client.set_service_url(self.config.url)
 36 |             
 37 |             # Ensure database exists
 38 |             self._ensure_database_exists()
 39 |             
 40 |         except Exception as e:
 41 |             print(f"Warning: Failed to initialize Cloudant client: {e}")
 42 |             self.client = None
 43 |     
 44 |     def _ensure_database_exists(self):
 45 |         """Create database if it doesn't exist"""
 46 |         try:
 47 |             self.client.get_database_information(db=self.database_name).get_result()
 48 |         except Exception:
 49 |             # Database doesn't exist, create it
 50 |             try:
 51 |                 self.client.put_database(db=self.database_name).get_result()
 52 |                 print(f"Created Cloudant database: {self.database_name}")
 53 |             except Exception as e:
 54 |                 print(f"Warning: Failed to create database: {e}")
 55 |     
 56 |     def save_modernization_session(
 57 |         self,
 58 |         user_id: str,
 59 |         filename: str,
 60 |         file_size: int,
 61 |         tables: List[Dict],
 62 |         transformation_log: List[Dict],
 63 |         processing_stats: Dict,
 64 |         generated_files: Optional[Dict[str, str]] = None,
 65 |         original_sql: Optional[str] = None
 66 |     ) -> Optional[str]:
 67 |         """
 68 |         Save a modernization session to Cloudant
 69 | 
 70 |         Args:
 71 |             user_id: Unique identifier for the user
 72 |             filename: Name of uploaded SQL file
 73 |             file_size: Size of uploaded file in bytes
 74 |             tables: List of parsed and normalized tables
 75 |             transformation_log: List of transformations applied
 76 |             processing_stats: Statistics about processing
 77 |             generated_files: Dictionary of generated file contents
 78 |             original_sql: Original SQL content uploaded by user
 79 | 
 80 |         Returns:
 81 |             Document ID if successful, None otherwise
 82 |         """
 83 |         if not self.client:
 84 |             return None
 85 |         
 86 |         try:
 87 |             session_id = str(uuid.uuid4())[:8]
 88 |             timestamp = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%f') + 'Z'
 89 |             
 90 |             document = {
 91 |                 '_id': f"session_{timestamp}_{session_id}",
 92 |                 'type': 'modernization_session',
 93 |                 'user_id': user_id,
 94 |                 'session_id': session_id,
 95 |                 'timestamp': timestamp,
 96 |                 'input': {
 97 |                     'filename': filename,
 98 |                     'file_size': file_size,
 99 |                     'upload_timestamp': timestamp
100 |                 },
101 |                 'processing': {
102 |                     'table_count': processing_stats.get('table_count', 0),
103 |                     'column_count': processing_stats.get('column_count', 0),
104 |                     'transformation_count': processing_stats.get('transformation_count', 0),
105 |                     'processing_time_ms': processing_stats.get('processing_time_ms', 0)
106 |                 },
107 |                 'output': {
108 |                     'tables': [
109 |                         {
110 |                             'original_name': table.get('original_name'),
111 |                             'clean_name': table.get('clean_name'),
112 |                             'table_name': table.get('table_name'),
113 |                             'column_count': len(table.get('columns', []))
114 |                         }
115 |                         for table in tables
116 |                     ],
117 |                     'generated_files': [
118 |                         'models.py',
119 |                         'database.py',
120 |                         'test_models.py',
121 |                         'README.md',
122 |                         'modernization_report.md',
123 |                         'requirements.txt',
124 |                         'functions.py',
125 |                         'indexes.py'
126 |                     ]
127 |                 },
128 |                 'transformations': transformation_log,
129 |                 'generated_file_contents': generated_files or {},
130 |                 'original_sql': original_sql or '',
131 |                 'metadata': {
132 |                     'app_version': '2.0.0',
133 |                     'bob_assisted': True,
134 |                     'success': True
135 |                 }
136 |             }
137 |             
138 |             response = self.client.post_document(
139 |                 db=self.database_name,
140 |                 document=Document.from_dict(document)
141 |             ).get_result()
142 |             
143 |             return response.get('id')
144 |             
145 |         except Exception as e:
146 |             print(f"Warning: Failed to save session to Cloudant: {e}")
147 |             return None
148 |     
149 |     def get_user_history(
150 |         self,
151 |         user_id: str,
152 |         limit: int = 10
153 |     ) -> List[Dict]:
154 |         """
155 |         Retrieve modernization history for a user
156 |         
157 |         Args:
158 |             user_id: Unique identifier for the user
159 |             limit: Maximum number of sessions to retrieve
160 |             
161 |         Returns:
162 |             List of session documents
163 |         """
164 |         if not self.client:
165 |             return []
166 |         
167 |         try:
168 |             # Query using Cloudant Query (Mango)
169 |             selector = {
170 |                 'type': 'modernization_session',
171 |                 'user_id': user_id
172 |             }
173 |             
174 |             response = self.client.post_find(
175 |                 db=self.database_name,
176 |                 selector=selector,
177 |                 sort=[{'timestamp': 'desc'}],
178 |                 limit=limit
179 |             ).get_result()
180 |             
181 |             return response.get('docs', [])
182 |             
183 |         except Exception as e:
184 |             print(f"Warning: Failed to retrieve history from Cloudant: {e}")
185 |             return []
186 |     
187 |     def get_session_by_id(self, session_id: str) -> Optional[Dict]:
188 |         """
189 |         Retrieve a specific session by ID
190 |         
191 |         Args:
192 |             session_id: Session identifier
193 |             
194 |         Returns:
195 |             Session document if found, None otherwise
196 |         """
197 |         if not self.client:
198 |             return None
199 |         
200 |         try:
201 |             selector = {
202 |                 'type': 'modernization_session',
203 |                 'session_id': session_id
204 |             }
205 |             
206 |             response = self.client.post_find(
207 |                 db=self.database_name,
208 |                 selector=selector,
209 |                 limit=1
210 |             ).get_result()
211 |             
212 |             docs = response.get('docs', [])
213 |             return docs[0] if docs else None
214 |             
215 |         except Exception as e:
216 |             print(f"Warning: Failed to retrieve session from Cloudant: {e}")
217 |             return None
218 |     
219 |     def get_statistics(self) -> Dict:
220 |         """
221 |         Get overall statistics from all sessions
222 |         
223 |         Returns:
224 |             Dictionary with aggregate statistics
225 |         """
226 |         if not self.client:
227 |             return {}
228 |         
229 |         try:
230 |             # Get all modernization sessions
231 |             selector = {
232 |                 'type': 'modernization_session'
233 |             }
234 |             
235 |             response = self.client.post_find(
236 |                 db=self.database_name,
237 |                 selector=selector,
238 |                 limit=1000  # Adjust based on expected volume
239 |             ).get_result()
240 |             
241 |             docs = response.get('docs', [])
242 |             
243 |             total_sessions = len(docs)
244 |             total_tables = sum(doc.get('processing', {}).get('table_count', 0) for doc in docs)
245 |             total_columns = sum(doc.get('processing', {}).get('column_count', 0) for doc in docs)
246 |             total_transformations = sum(doc.get('processing', {}).get('transformation_count', 0) for doc in docs)
247 |             
248 |             return {
249 |                 'total_sessions': total_sessions,
250 |                 'total_tables_processed': total_tables,
251 |                 'total_columns_normalized': total_columns,
252 |                 'total_transformations': total_transformations,
253 |                 'unique_users': len(set(doc.get('user_id') for doc in docs))
254 |             }
255 |             
256 |         except Exception as e:
257 |             print(f"Warning: Failed to retrieve statistics from Cloudant: {e}")
258 |             return {}
259 | 
260 | # Made with Bob
</content>

  1 | # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  2 | # LegacyLink AI - Environment Configuration
  3 | # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  4 | # This file contains environment variables for optional features.
  5 | # Copy this file to .env and fill in your actual values.
  6 | #
  7 | # IMPORTANT: Never commit .env to version control!
  8 | # The .env file is already in .gitignore for your protection.
  9 | # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
 10 | 
 11 | # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 12 | # IBM Cloudant NoSQL Database Configuration (Optional)
 13 | # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 14 | # Enable session history tracking with IBM Cloudant.
 15 | # This feature is completely optional - the app works without it.
 16 | #
 17 | # Setup Instructions:
 18 | # 1. Create IBM Cloud account: https://cloud.ibm.com
 19 | # 2. Create Cloudant service (Lite plan is free)
 20 | # 3. Generate service credentials with Manager role
 21 | # 4. Copy URL and API key below
 22 | # 5. Restart the application
 23 | #
 24 | # Detailed Guide: See docs/CLOUDANT_SETUP.md
 25 | # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 26 | 
 27 | # Cloudant Service URL
 28 | # Format: https://YOUR-INSTANCE-NAME.cloudantnosqldb.appdomain.cloud
 29 | # Example: https://abc123-bluemix.cloudantnosqldb.appdomain.cloud
 30 | # Find this in: IBM Cloud → Cloudant Instance → Service Credentials → url
 31 | CLOUDANT_URL=https://your-instance.cloudantnosqldb.appdomain.cloud
 32 | 
 33 | # Cloudant IAM API Key
 34 | # Format: Long alphanumeric string (40+ characters)
 35 | # Example: AbCdEf123456789_GhIjKlMnOpQrStUvWxYz0123456789
 36 | # Find this in: IBM Cloud → Cloudant Instance → Service Credentials → apikey
 37 | # SECURITY: Keep this secret! Never commit to git or share publicly.
 38 | CLOUDANT_APIKEY=your-api-key-here
 39 | 
 40 | # Database Name
 41 | # The name of the database to store session history.
 42 | # Default: legacylink_history
 43 | # Note: Database will be created automatically if it doesn't exist.
 44 | CLOUDANT_DATABASE_NAME=legacylink_history
 45 | 
 46 | # Enable/Disable History Tracking
 47 | # Set to 'true' to enable session history tracking
 48 | # Set to 'false' to disable (app will work normally without Cloudant)
 49 | # Default: true
 50 | ENABLE_HISTORY_TRACKING=true
 51 | 
 52 | # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 53 | # Verification
 54 | # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 55 | # After configuration, verify your setup:
 56 | #
 57 | # 1. Run tests:
 58 | #    pytest tests/test_cloudant_integration.py -v
 59 | #
 60 | # 2. Start app:
 61 | #    streamlit run app.py
 62 | #
 63 | # 3. Upload a SQL file and look for:
 64 | #    "✅ Session saved to history (ID: ...)"
 65 | #
 66 | # 4. Check Cloudant dashboard:
 67 | #    IBM Cloud → Cloudant → Launch Dashboard → legacylink_history
 68 | # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 69 | 
 70 | # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 71 | # Troubleshooting
 72 | # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 73 | # Issue: "Could not save to history" warning
 74 | # Solution: Check CLOUDANT_URL and CLOUDANT_APIKEY are correct
 75 | #
 76 | # Issue: "Authentication failed"
 77 | # Solution: Regenerate service credentials with Manager role
 78 | #
 79 | # Issue: App works but no history saved
 80 | # Solution: Ensure ENABLE_HISTORY_TRACKING=true and restart app
 81 | #
 82 | # Full troubleshooting guide: docs/CLOUDANT_SETUP.md#troubleshooting
 83 | # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 84 | 
 85 | # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 86 | # Cost Information
 87 | # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 88 | # IBM Cloudant Lite Plan (Free):
 89 | # - 1 GB storage
 90 | # - 20 lookups/second
 91 | # - 10 writes/second
 92 | # - Perfect for development and demos
 93 | #
 94 | # Typical Usage:
 95 | # - Each session: ~5-50 KB
 96 | # - 100 sessions/day: ~75 MB/month
 97 | # - Free tier is sufficient for most use cases
 98 | # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 99 | 
100 | # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
101 | # Future Configuration Options
102 | # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
103 | # Additional environment variables can be added here as the project grows:
104 | # - API keys for other services
105 | # - Feature flags
106 | # - Performance tuning parameters
107 | # - Logging configuration
108 | # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
</content>

<file><path>docs/CLOUDANT_SETUP.md</path>
<content lines="1-366">
  1 | # IBM Cloudant Setup Guide for LegacyLink AI
  2 | 
  3 | This guide walks you through setting up IBM Cloudant NoSQL database for session history tracking in LegacyLink AI.
  4 | 
  5 | ## Table of Contents
  6 | 
  7 | - [Overview](#overview)
  8 | - [Prerequisites](#prerequisites)
  9 | - [Step 1: Create IBM Cloud Account](#step-1-create-ibm-cloud-account)
 10 | - [Step 2: Create Cloudant Service Instance](#step-2-create-cloudant-service-instance)
 11 | - [Step 3: Generate Service Credentials](#step-3-generate-service-credentials)
 12 | - [Step 4: Configure LegacyLink AI](#step-4-configure-legacylink-ai)
 13 | - [Step 5: Verify Connection](#step-5-verify-connection)
 14 | - [Troubleshooting](#troubleshooting)
 15 | - [Security Best Practices](#security-best-practices)
 16 | 
 17 | ---
 18 | 
 19 | ## Overview
 20 | 
 21 | IBM Cloudant is a fully managed NoSQL database service that LegacyLink AI uses to store modernization session history. This feature is **optional** - the application works perfectly without it, but enabling it provides:
 22 | 
 23 | - **Session History**: Track all SQL modernization sessions
 24 | - **User Analytics**: View processing statistics across sessions
 25 | - **Audit Trail**: Maintain records of transformations applied
 26 | - **Session Replay**: Retrieve and review previous modernizations
 27 | 
 28 | ---
 29 | 
 30 | ## Prerequisites
 31 | 
 32 | - IBM Cloud account (free tier available)
 33 | - LegacyLink AI application installed
 34 | - Basic command line knowledge
 35 | 
 36 | ---
 37 | 
 38 | ## Step 1: Create IBM Cloud Account
 39 | 
 40 | 1. **Visit IBM Cloud**: Go to [https://cloud.ibm.com/registration](https://cloud.ibm.com/registration)
 41 | 
 42 | 2. **Sign Up**: Create a free IBM Cloud account
 43 |    - Enter your email address
 44 |    - Verify your email
 45 |    - Complete the registration form
 46 | 
 47 | 3. **Login**: Access the IBM Cloud dashboard at [https://cloud.ibm.com](https://cloud.ibm.com)
 48 | 
 49 | > **Note**: IBM Cloud offers a free tier that includes Cloudant Lite plan with 1GB storage and 20 lookups/sec.
 50 | 
 51 | ---
 52 | 
 53 | ## Step 2: Create Cloudant Service Instance
 54 | 
 55 | 1. **Navigate to Catalog**:
 56 |    - Click **Catalog** in the top navigation
 57 |    - Search for "Cloudant"
 58 |    - Select **Cloudant** from the results
 59 | 
 60 | 2. **Configure Service**:
 61 |    - **Region**: Select your preferred region (e.g., `us-south`, `eu-gb`)
 62 |    - **Pricing Plan**: Select **Lite** (free) or **Standard** (paid)
 63 |    - **Service Name**: Enter a name (e.g., `legacylink-cloudant`)
 64 |    - **Resource Group**: Select `Default` or create a new one
 65 | 
 66 | 3. **Create Instance**:
 67 |    - Review your configuration
 68 |    - Click **Create**
 69 |    - Wait for provisioning to complete (usually < 1 minute)
 70 | 
 71 | 4. **Access Dashboard**:
 72 |    - Click on your newly created Cloudant instance
 73 |    - You'll see the service dashboard
 74 | 
 75 | ---
 76 | 
 77 | ## Step 3: Generate Service Credentials
 78 | 
 79 | 1. **Open Service Credentials**:
 80 |    - In your Cloudant instance dashboard
 81 |    - Click **Service credentials** in the left sidebar
 82 | 
 83 | 2. **Create New Credential**:
 84 |    - Click **New credential** button
 85 |    - **Name**: Enter `legacylink-credentials`
 86 |    - **Role**: Select **Manager** (full access)
 87 |    - Click **Add**
 88 | 
 89 | 3. **View Credentials**:
 90 |    - Click **View credentials** next to your new credential
 91 |    - You'll see a JSON object with connection details
 92 | 
 93 | 4. **Copy Required Values**:
 94 |    ```json
 95 |    {
 96 |      "apikey": "YOUR_API_KEY_HERE",
 97 |      "url": "https://YOUR_INSTANCE.cloudantnosqldb.appdomain.cloud",
 98 |      ...
 99 |    }
100 |    ```
101 |    
102 |    Copy these two values:
103 |    - `apikey`: Your IAM API key
104 |    - `url`: Your Cloudant service URL
105 | 
106 | > **Security Warning**: Keep these credentials secure! Never commit them to version control.
107 | 
108 | ---
109 | 
110 | ## Step 4: Configure LegacyLink AI
111 | 
112 | ### Option A: Using Environment Variables (Recommended)
113 | 
114 | 1. **Create `.env` file** in your project root:
115 |    ```bash
116 |    # Navigate to project directory
117 |    cd /path/to/Try-Tri-Hard-Bob-AI-Hack
118 |    
119 |    # Copy example file
120 |    cp .env.example .env
121 |    ```
122 | 
123 | 2. **Edit `.env` file**:
124 |    ```bash
125 |    # IBM Cloudant Configuration
126 |    CLOUDANT_URL=https://YOUR_INSTANCE.cloudantnosqldb.appdomain.cloud
127 |    CLOUDANT_APIKEY=YOUR_API_KEY_HERE
128 |    CLOUDANT_DATABASE_NAME=legacylink_history
129 |    
130 |    # Enable history tracking
131 |    ENABLE_HISTORY_TRACKING=true
132 |    ```
133 | 
134 | 3. **Replace placeholders**:
135 |    - Replace `YOUR_INSTANCE` with your actual instance name
136 |    - Replace `YOUR_API_KEY_HERE` with your actual API key
137 |    - Keep `CLOUDANT_DATABASE_NAME` as `legacylink_history` (or customize)
138 | 
139 | ### Option B: Using System Environment Variables
140 | 
141 | **Windows (PowerShell)**:
142 | ```powershell
143 | $env:CLOUDANT_URL="https://YOUR_INSTANCE.cloudantnosqldb.appdomain.cloud"
144 | $env:CLOUDANT_APIKEY="YOUR_API_KEY_HERE"
145 | $env:CLOUDANT_DATABASE_NAME="legacylink_history"
146 | $env:ENABLE_HISTORY_TRACKING="true"
147 | ```
148 | 
149 | **Linux/macOS (Bash)**:
150 | ```bash
151 | export CLOUDANT_URL="https://YOUR_INSTANCE.cloudantnosqldb.appdomain.cloud"
152 | export CLOUDANT_APIKEY="YOUR_API_KEY_HERE"
153 | export CLOUDANT_DATABASE_NAME="legacylink_history"
154 | export ENABLE_HISTORY_TRACKING="true"
155 | ```
156 | 
157 | ---
158 | 
159 | ## Step 5: Verify Connection
160 | 
161 | 1. **Run Tests**:
162 |    ```bash
163 |    # Run Cloudant integration tests
164 |    pytest tests/test_cloudant_integration.py -v
165 |    ```
166 |    
167 |    Expected output:
168 |    ```
169 |    ============================= test session starts =============================
170 |    ...
171 |    tests/test_cloudant_integration.py::test_cloudant_config_validation PASSED
172 |    tests/test_cloudant_integration.py::test_save_modernization_session PASSED
173 |    ...
174 |    ============================= 12 passed in 0.46s ==============================
175 |    ```
176 | 
177 | 2. **Start Application**:
178 |    ```bash
179 |    streamlit run app.py
180 |    ```
181 | 
182 | 3. **Check Startup Messages**:
183 |    - Look for Cloudant initialization messages in the console
184 |    - No errors should appear related to Cloudant
185 | 
186 | 4. **Test Session Saving**:
187 |    - Upload a sample SQL file
188 |    - Click "PROCESS SQL FILE"
189 |    - Look for success message: "✅ Session saved to history (ID: ...)"
190 | 
191 | 5. **Verify in Cloudant Dashboard**:
192 |    - Go to IBM Cloud dashboard
193 |    - Open your Cloudant instance
194 |    - Click **Launch Dashboard**
195 |    - Select `legacylink_history` database
196 |    - You should see session documents
197 | 
198 | ---
199 | 
200 | ## Troubleshooting
201 | 
202 | ### Issue: "Could not save to history" Warning
203 | 
204 | **Possible Causes**:
205 | - Invalid API key or URL
206 | - Network connectivity issues
207 | - Database doesn't exist (should auto-create)
208 | 
209 | **Solutions**:
210 | 1. Verify credentials in `.env` file
211 | 2. Check network connection
212 | 3. Ensure API key has Manager role
213 | 4. Check Cloudant service status in IBM Cloud
214 | 
215 | ### Issue: Database Not Created Automatically
216 | 
217 | **Solution**:
218 | 1. Manually create database in Cloudant dashboard:
219 |    - Click **Create Database**
220 |    - Name: `legacylink_history`
221 |    - Partitioning: **Non-partitioned**
222 |    - Click **Create**
223 | 
224 | ### Issue: "Authentication failed" Error
225 | 
226 | **Solution**:
227 | 1. Regenerate service credentials:
228 |    - Delete old credential
229 |    - Create new credential with Manager role
230 |    - Update `.env` file with new values
231 | 
232 | ### Issue: Application Works But No History Saved
233 | 
234 | **Check**:
235 | 1. Verify `ENABLE_HISTORY_TRACKING=true` in `.env`
236 | 2. Ensure `.env` file is in project root
237 | 3. Restart application after changing `.env`
238 | 4. Check console for warning messages
239 | 
240 | ### Issue: Rate Limit Exceeded (Lite Plan)
241 | 
242 | **Symptoms**: "429 Too Many Requests" errors
243 | 
244 | **Solutions**:
245 | - Upgrade to Standard plan for higher limits
246 | - Reduce processing frequency
247 | - Implement request throttling
248 | 
249 | ---
250 | 
251 | ## Security Best Practices
252 | 
253 | ### 1. Protect Credentials
254 | 
255 | ✅ **DO**:
256 | - Store credentials in `.env` file (not committed to git)
257 | - Use environment variables in production
258 | - Rotate API keys regularly
259 | - Use IAM roles with minimum required permissions
260 | 
261 | ❌ **DON'T**:
262 | - Commit `.env` file to version control
263 | - Share credentials in chat/email
264 | - Use root/admin credentials
265 | - Hardcode credentials in source code
266 | 
267 | ### 2. Network Security
268 | 
269 | - Use HTTPS only (Cloudant enforces this)
270 | - Restrict access by IP if possible
271 | - Use VPC for production deployments
272 | - Enable audit logging in IBM Cloud
273 | 
274 | ### 3. Data Privacy
275 | 
276 | - Review data retention policies
277 | - Implement data encryption at rest (enabled by default)
278 | - Consider GDPR/compliance requirements
279 | - Document what data is stored
280 | 
281 | ### 4. Access Control
282 | 
283 | - Use separate credentials for dev/prod
284 | - Implement least privilege principle
285 | - Regularly audit access logs
286 | - Revoke unused credentials
287 | 
288 | ---
289 | 
290 | ## Database Schema
291 | 
292 | The `legacylink_history` database stores documents with this structure:
293 | 
294 | ```json
295 | {
296 |   "_id": "session_2026-05-17T02:43:00.000000Z_abc12345",
297 |   "type": "modernization_session",
298 |   "user_id": "user-identifier",
299 |   "session_id": "abc12345",
300 |   "timestamp": "2026-05-17T02:43:00.000000Z",
301 |   "input": {
302 |     "filename": "legacy_schema.sql",
303 |     "file_size": 1024,
304 |     "upload_timestamp": "2026-05-17T02:43:00.000000Z"
305 |   },
306 |   "processing": {
307 |     "table_count": 3,
308 |     "column_count": 25,
309 |     "transformation_count": 50,
310 |     "processing_time_ms": 500
311 |   },
312 |   "output": {
313 |     "tables": [...],
314 |     "generated_files": [...]
315 |   },
316 |   "transformations": [...],
317 |   "metadata": {
318 |     "app_version": "1.0.0",
319 |     "bob_assisted": true,
320 |     "success": true
321 |   }
322 | }
323 | ```
324 | 
325 | ---
326 | 
327 | ## Cost Estimation
328 | 
329 | ### Lite Plan (Free)
330 | - **Storage**: 1 GB
331 | - **Throughput**: 20 lookups/sec, 10 writes/sec
332 | - **Cost**: $0/month
333 | - **Best for**: Development, testing, small projects
334 | 
335 | ### Standard Plan (Paid)
336 | - **Storage**: $1.00/GB/month
337 | - **Throughput**: Provisioned capacity units
338 | - **Cost**: Variable based on usage
339 | - **Best for**: Production, high-volume applications
340 | 
341 | **Typical Usage for LegacyLink AI**:
342 | - Each session: ~5-50 KB
343 | - 100 sessions/day: ~2.5 MB/day = ~75 MB/month
344 | - **Estimated cost**: Free tier sufficient for most use cases
345 | 
346 | ---
347 | 
348 | ## Next Steps
349 | 
350 | 1. ✅ Cloudant is configured and working
351 | 2. 📖 Read [CLOUDANT_INTEGRATION.md](CLOUDANT_INTEGRATION.md) for technical details
352 | 3. 🧪 Run integration tests regularly
353 | 4. 📊 Monitor usage in IBM Cloud dashboard
354 | 5. 🔒 Review security settings periodically
355 | 
356 | ---
357 | 
358 | ## Support
359 | 
360 | - **IBM Cloud Support**: [https://cloud.ibm.com/unifiedsupport/supportcenter](https://cloud.ibm.com/unifiedsupport/supportcenter)
361 | - **Cloudant Documentation**: [https://cloud.ibm.com/docs/Cloudant](https://cloud.ibm.com/docs/Cloudant)
362 | - **LegacyLink AI Issues**: [GitHub Issues](https://github.com/your-repo/issues)
363 | 
364 | ---
365 | 
366 | **Made with IBM Bob** 🤖
</content>

<file><path>docs/CLOUDANT_INTEGRATION.md</path>
<content lines="1-781">
  1 | # IBM Cloudant Integration Technical Documentation
  2 | 
  3 | This document provides technical details about the IBM Cloudant NoSQL database integration in LegacyLink AI.
  4 | 
  5 | ## Table of Contents
  6 | 
  7 | - [Architecture Overview](#architecture-overview)
  8 | - [Data Flow](#data-flow)
  9 | - [Component Details](#component-details)
 10 | - [API Reference](#api-reference)
 11 | - [Session Document Schema](#session-document-schema)
 12 | - [Query Patterns](#query-patterns)
 13 | - [Error Handling](#error-handling)
 14 | - [Performance Considerations](#performance-considerations)
 15 | - [Testing](#testing)
 16 | - [Development Guidelines](#development-guidelines)
 17 | 
 18 | ---
 19 | 
 20 | ## Architecture Overview
 21 | 
 22 | LegacyLink AI uses IBM Cloudant as an optional NoSQL database for storing modernization session history. The integration follows a layered architecture:
 23 | 
 24 | ```
 25 | ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
 26 | │                     Streamlit UI (app.py)                   │
 27 | │  - File upload handling                                     │
 28 | │  - User interaction                                         │
 29 | │  - Session state management                                 │
 30 | └────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────┘
 31 |                          │
 32 |                          ▼
 33 | ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
 34 | │              Application Layer (app.py)                     │
 35 | │  - save_to_cloudant()                                       │
 36 | │  - display_user_history()                                   │
 37 | │  - Session data preparation                                 │
 38 | └────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────┘
 39 |                          │
 40 |                          ▼
 41 | ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
 42 | │         Storage Layer (storage/cloudant_client.py)          │
 43 | │  - CloudantClient class                                     │
 44 | │  - CRUD operations                                          │
 45 | │  - Query methods                                            │
 46 | └────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────┘
 47 |                          │
 48 |                          ▼
 49 | ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
 50 | │      Configuration Layer (config/cloudant_config.py)        │
 51 | │  - CloudantConfig class                                     │
 52 | │  - Environment variable loading                             │
 53 | │  - Validation logic                                         │
 54 | └────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────┘
 55 |                          │
 56 |                          ▼
 57 | ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
 58 | │              IBM Cloudant NoSQL Database                    │
 59 | │  - Document storage                                         │
 60 | │  - Indexing and querying                                    │
 61 | │  - Replication and backup                                   │
 62 | └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
 63 | ```
 64 | 
 65 | ### Design Principles
 66 | 
 67 | 1. **Optional Integration**: App works without Cloudant configured
 68 | 2. **Graceful Degradation**: Failures don't break the main workflow
 69 | 3. **Separation of Concerns**: Clear layer boundaries
 70 | 4. **Testability**: Fully mocked for unit testing
 71 | 5. **Security**: Credentials via environment variables only
 72 | 
 73 | ---
 74 | 
 75 | ## Data Flow
 76 | 
 77 | ### Session Save Flow
 78 | 
 79 | ```mermaid
 80 | sequenceDiagram
 81 |     participant User
 82 |     participant UI as Streamlit UI
 83 |     participant App as app.py
 84 |     participant Client as CloudantClient
 85 |     participant DB as Cloudant DB
 86 | 
 87 |     User->>UI: Upload SQL file
 88 |     User->>UI: Click "PROCESS SQL FILE"
 89 |     UI->>App: process_sql_file()
 90 |     App->>App: Parse SQL
 91 |     App->>App: Normalize names
 92 |     App->>App: Generate models
 93 |     App->>App: Create result dict
 94 |     App->>App: save_to_cloudant(result)
 95 |     
 96 |     alt Cloudant configured
 97 |         App->>Client: save_modernization_session()
 98 |         Client->>Client: Generate session_id
 99 |         Client->>Client: Format timestamp
100 |         Client->>Client: Build document
101 |         Client->>DB: post_document()
102 |         DB-->>Client: {id, rev}
103 |         Client-->>App: doc_id
104 |         App->>UI: Show success message
105 |     else Cloudant not configured
106 |         App->>App: Skip silently
107 |     end
108 |     
109 |     App->>UI: st.rerun()
110 |     UI->>User: Display results
111 | ```
112 | 
113 | ### History Retrieval Flow
114 | 
115 | ```mermaid
116 | sequenceDiagram
117 |     participant User
118 |     participant UI as Streamlit UI
119 |     participant App as app.py
120 |     participant Client as CloudantClient
121 |     participant DB as Cloudant DB
122 | 
123 |     User->>UI: Navigate to History
124 |     UI->>App: display_user_history()
125 |     
126 |     alt Cloudant configured
127 |         App->>Client: get_user_history(user_id)
128 |         Client->>DB: post_find(selector)
129 |         DB-->>Client: {docs: [...]}
130 |         Client-->>App: history[]
131 |         App->>UI: Render history items
132 |         UI->>User: Display sessions
133 |     else Cloudant not configured
134 |         App->>UI: Show info message
135 |         UI->>User: "History tracking not enabled"
136 |     end
137 | ```
138 | 
139 | ---
140 | 
141 | ## Component Details
142 | 
143 | ### 1. CloudantConfig (`config/cloudant_config.py`)
144 | 
145 | **Purpose**: Manages Cloudant connection configuration
146 | 
147 | **Key Methods**:
148 | - `__init__()`: Loads environment variables
149 | - `is_configured()`: Validates configuration completeness
150 | - `get_connection_params()`: Returns connection dictionary
151 | 
152 | **Environment Variables**:
153 | ```python
154 | CLOUDANT_URL              # Required: Service URL
155 | CLOUDANT_APIKEY           # Required: IAM API key
156 | CLOUDANT_DATABASE_NAME    # Optional: Default 'legacylink_history'
157 | ENABLE_HISTORY_TRACKING   # Optional: Default 'true'
158 | ```
159 | 
160 | **Example Usage**:
161 | ```python
162 | from config.cloudant_config import CloudantConfig
163 | 
164 | config = CloudantConfig()
165 | if config.is_configured():
166 |     print(f"Cloudant URL: {config.url}")
167 |     print(f"Database: {config.database_name}")
168 | ```
169 | 
170 | ---
171 | 
172 | ### 2. CloudantClient (`storage/cloudant_client.py`)
173 | 
174 | **Purpose**: Handles all Cloudant database operations
175 | 
176 | **Initialization**:
177 | ```python
178 | from storage.cloudant_client import CloudantClient
179 | from config.cloudant_config import CloudantConfig
180 | 
181 | config = CloudantConfig()
182 | client = CloudantClient(config) if config.is_configured() else None
183 | ```
184 | 
185 | **Key Features**:
186 | - Automatic database creation
187 | - IAM authentication
188 | - Error handling with graceful degradation
189 | - Query optimization
190 | 
191 | ---
192 | 
193 | ### 3. Application Integration (`app.py`)
194 | 
195 | **Initialization** (lines 131-132):
196 | ```python
197 | cloudant_config = CloudantConfig()
198 | cloudant_client = CloudantClient(cloudant_config) if cloudant_config.is_configured() else None
199 | ```
200 | 
201 | **Session Saving** (line 879):
202 | ```python
203 | # After successful SQL processing
204 | save_to_cloudant(result, st.session_state.user_id)
205 | ```
206 | 
207 | **Helper Function** (lines 134-161):
208 | ```python
209 | def save_to_cloudant(result: dict, user_id: str):
210 |     """Save modernization session to Cloudant"""
211 |     if not cloudant_client:
212 |         return None
213 |     
214 |     try:
215 |         doc_id = cloudant_client.save_modernization_session(
216 |             user_id=user_id,
217 |             filename=result.get('filename', 'unknown.sql'),
218 |             file_size=result.get('file_size', 0),
219 |             tables=result.get('tables', []),
220 |             transformation_log=result.get('transformation_log', []),
221 |             processing_stats={...}
222 |         )
223 |         
224 |         if doc_id:
225 |             st.success(f"✅ Session saved to history (ID: {doc_id[:16]}...)")
226 |         
227 |         return doc_id
228 |         
229 |     except Exception as e:
230 |         st.warning(f"Could not save to history: {e}")
231 |         return None
232 | ```
233 | 
234 | ---
235 | 
236 | ## API Reference
237 | 
238 | ### CloudantClient Methods
239 | 
240 | #### `save_modernization_session()`
241 | 
242 | Saves a modernization session to Cloudant.
243 | 
244 | **Signature**:
245 | ```python
246 | def save_modernization_session(
247 |     self,
248 |     user_id: str,
249 |     filename: str,
250 |     file_size: int,
251 |     tables: List[Dict],
252 |     transformation_log: List[Dict],
253 |     processing_stats: Dict
254 | ) -> Optional[str]
255 | ```
256 | 
257 | **Parameters**:
258 | - `user_id` (str): Unique identifier for the user
259 | - `filename` (str): Name of uploaded SQL file
260 | - `file_size` (int): Size of file in bytes
261 | - `tables` (List[Dict]): Parsed and normalized table definitions
262 | - `transformation_log` (List[Dict]): List of transformations applied
263 | - `processing_stats` (Dict): Processing metrics
264 | 
265 | **Returns**:
266 | - `str`: Document ID if successful
267 | - `None`: If Cloudant not configured or error occurred
268 | 
269 | **Example**:
270 | ```python
271 | doc_id = client.save_modernization_session(
272 |     user_id="user123",
273 |     filename="legacy_schema.sql",
274 |     file_size=2048,
275 |     tables=[{
276 |         'original_name': 'tbl_CUST',
277 |         'clean_name': 'Customer',
278 |         'table_name': 'customers',
279 |         'columns': [...]
280 |     }],
281 |     transformation_log=[{
282 |         'type': 'table_rename',
283 |         'original': 'tbl_CUST',
284 |         'normalized': 'Customer'
285 |     }],
286 |     processing_stats={
287 |         'table_count': 3,
288 |         'column_count': 25,
289 |         'transformation_count': 50,
290 |         'processing_time_ms': 500
291 |     }
292 | )
293 | ```
294 | 
295 | ---
296 | 
297 | #### `get_user_history()`
298 | 
299 | Retrieves modernization history for a specific user.
300 | 
301 | **Signature**:
302 | ```python
303 | def get_user_history(
304 |     self,
305 |     user_id: str,
306 |     limit: int = 10
307 | ) -> List[Dict]
308 | ```
309 | 
310 | **Parameters**:
311 | - `user_id` (str): User identifier
312 | - `limit` (int): Maximum number of sessions to retrieve (default: 10)
313 | 
314 | **Returns**:
315 | - `List[Dict]`: List of session documents, sorted by timestamp (newest first)
316 | - `[]`: Empty list if no history or Cloudant not configured
317 | 
318 | **Example**:
319 | ```python
320 | history = client.get_user_history("user123", limit=5)
321 | for session in history:
322 |     print(f"File: {session['input']['filename']}")
323 |     print(f"Tables: {session['processing']['table_count']}")
324 |     print(f"Time: {session['timestamp']}")
325 | ```
326 | 
327 | ---
328 | 
329 | #### `get_session_by_id()`
330 | 
331 | Retrieves a specific session by its session ID.
332 | 
333 | **Signature**:
334 | ```python
335 | def get_session_by_id(self, session_id: str) -> Optional[Dict]
336 | ```
337 | 
338 | **Parameters**:
339 | - `session_id` (str): 8-character session identifier
340 | 
341 | **Returns**:
342 | - `Dict`: Session document if found
343 | - `None`: If not found or Cloudant not configured
344 | 
345 | **Example**:
346 | ```python
347 | session = client.get_session_by_id("abc12345")
348 | if session:
349 |     print(f"Found session from {session['timestamp']}")
350 | ```
351 | 
352 | ---
353 | 
354 | #### `get_statistics()`
355 | 
356 | Retrieves aggregate statistics across all sessions.
357 | 
358 | **Signature**:
359 | ```python
360 | def get_statistics(self) -> Dict
361 | ```
362 | 
363 | **Returns**:
364 | - `Dict`: Statistics dictionary with keys:
365 |   - `total_sessions`: Total number of sessions
366 |   - `total_tables_processed`: Sum of all tables processed
367 |   - `total_columns_normalized`: Sum of all columns normalized
368 |   - `total_transformations`: Sum of all transformations
369 |   - `unique_users`: Count of unique user IDs
370 | - `{}`: Empty dict if Cloudant not configured
371 | 
372 | **Example**:
373 | ```python
374 | stats = client.get_statistics()
375 | print(f"Total sessions: {stats['total_sessions']}")
376 | print(f"Unique users: {stats['unique_users']}")
377 | ```
378 | 
379 | ---
380 | 
381 | ## Session Document Schema
382 | 
383 | ### Document Structure
384 | 
385 | ```json
386 | {
387 |   "_id": "session_2026-05-17T02:43:00.000000Z_abc12345",
388 |   "_rev": "1-abc123...",
389 |   "type": "modernization_session",
390 |   "user_id": "user-identifier",
391 |   "session_id": "abc12345",
392 |   "timestamp": "2026-05-17T02:43:00.000000Z",
393 |   
394 |   "input": {
395 |     "filename": "legacy_customer_schema.sql",
396 |     "file_size": 2048,
397 |     "upload_timestamp": "2026-05-17T02:43:00.000000Z"
398 |   },
399 |   
400 |   "processing": {
401 |     "table_count": 3,
402 |     "column_count": 25,
403 |     "transformation_count": 50,
404 |     "processing_time_ms": 500
405 |   },
406 |   
407 |   "output": {
408 |     "tables": [
409 |       {
410 |         "original_name": "tbl_CUST_MSTR_2012_v2",
411 |         "clean_name": "Customer",
412 |         "table_name": "customers",
413 |         "column_count": 8
414 |       }
415 |     ],
416 |     "generated_files": [
417 |       "models.py",
418 |       "database.py",
419 |       "test_models.py",
420 |       "README.md",
421 |       "modernization_report.md",
422 |       "requirements.txt"
423 |     ]
424 |   },
425 |   
426 |   "transformations": [
427 |     {
428 |       "type": "table_rename",
429 |       "original": "tbl_CUST_MSTR_2012_v2",
430 |       "normalized": "Customer",
431 |       "reason": "Removed prefix and version suffix"
432 |     },
433 |     {
434 |       "type": "column_rename",
435 |       "table": "Customer",
436 |       "original": "vch_fname",
437 |       "normalized": "first_name",
438 |       "reason": "Expanded abbreviation and removed type prefix"
439 |     }
440 |   ],
441 |   
442 |   "metadata": {
443 |     "app_version": "1.0.0",
444 |     "bob_assisted": true,
445 |     "success": true
446 |   }
447 | }
448 | ```
449 | 
450 | ### Field Descriptions
451 | 
452 | | Field | Type | Description |
453 | |-------|------|-------------|
454 | | `_id` | string | Unique document ID (format: `session_{timestamp}_{session_id}`) |
455 | | `_rev` | string | Cloudant revision ID (managed by Cloudant) |
456 | | `type` | string | Document type identifier (always `"modernization_session"`) |
457 | | `user_id` | string | User identifier (from session state) |
458 | | `session_id` | string | 8-character unique session identifier |
459 | | `timestamp` | string | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp of session creation |
460 | | `input.filename` | string | Original SQL filename |
461 | | `input.file_size` | integer | File size in bytes |
462 | | `input.upload_timestamp` | string | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp of upload |
463 | | `processing.table_count` | integer | Number of tables processed |
464 | | `processing.column_count` | integer | Total columns across all tables |
465 | | `processing.transformation_count` | integer | Number of transformations applied |
466 | | `processing.processing_time_ms` | integer | Processing time in milliseconds |
467 | | `output.tables` | array | Simplified table information |
468 | | `output.generated_files` | array | List of generated file names |
469 | | `transformations` | array | Detailed transformation log |
470 | | `metadata.app_version` | string | Application version |
471 | | `metadata.bob_assisted` | boolean | Whether IBM Bob was used |
472 | | `metadata.success` | boolean | Whether processing succeeded |
473 | 
474 | ---
475 | 
476 | ## Query Patterns
477 | 
478 | ### 1. Find User Sessions
479 | 
480 | ```python
481 | selector = {
482 |     'type': 'modernization_session',
483 |     'user_id': 'user123'
484 | }
485 | 
486 | response = client.client.post_find(
487 |     db=database_name,
488 |     selector=selector,
489 |     sort=[{'timestamp': 'desc'}],
490 |     limit=10
491 | ).get_result()
492 | 
493 | sessions = response.get('docs', [])
494 | ```
495 | 
496 | ### 2. Find Sessions by Date Range
497 | 
498 | ```python
499 | selector = {
500 |     'type': 'modernization_session',
501 |     'timestamp': {
502 |         '$gte': '2026-05-01T00:00:00.000000Z',
503 |         '$lte': '2026-05-31T23:59:59.999999Z'
504 |     }
505 | }
506 | ```
507 | 
508 | ### 3. Find Sessions with High Table Count
509 | 
510 | ```python
511 | selector = {
512 |     'type': 'modernization_session',
513 |     'processing.table_count': {'$gte': 10}
514 | }
515 | ```
516 | 
517 | ### 4. Aggregate Statistics
518 | 
519 | ```python
520 | # Get all sessions
521 | selector = {'type': 'modernization_session'}
522 | response = client.client.post_find(
523 |     db=database_name,
524 |     selector=selector,
525 |     limit=1000
526 | ).get_result()
527 | 
528 | docs = response.get('docs', [])
529 | 
530 | # Calculate aggregates
531 | total_tables = sum(doc.get('processing', {}).get('table_count', 0) for doc in docs)
532 | avg_processing_time = sum(doc.get('processing', {}).get('processing_time_ms', 0) for doc in docs) / len(docs)
533 | ```
534 | 
535 | ---
536 | 
537 | ## Error Handling
538 | 
539 | ### Strategy
540 | 
541 | The integration uses **graceful degradation**:
542 | 
543 | 1. **Configuration Errors**: App continues without Cloudant
544 | 2. **Connection Errors**: Warning shown, processing continues
545 | 3. **Save Errors**: Warning shown, user can still download results
546 | 4. **Query Errors**: Empty results returned, no crash
547 | 
548 | ### Error Scenarios
549 | 
550 | #### 1. Cloudant Not Configured
551 | 
552 | ```python
553 | if not cloudant_client:
554 |     st.info("💡 History tracking is not enabled. Configure Cloudant to enable this feature.")
555 |     return
556 | ```
557 | 
558 | **User Impact**: No history tracking, app works normally
559 | 
560 | #### 2. Authentication Failure
561 | 
562 | ```python
563 | try:
564 |     authenticator = IAMAuthenticator(self.config.apikey)
565 |     self.client = CloudantV1(authenticator=authenticator)
566 | except Exception as e:
567 |     print(f"Warning: Failed to initialize Cloudant client: {e}")
568 |     self.client = None
569 | ```
570 | 
571 | **User Impact**: Warning in console, app continues
572 | 
573 | #### 3. Save Failure
574 | 
575 | ```python
576 | try:
577 |     doc_id = cloudant_client.save_modernization_session(...)
578 |     if doc_id:
579 |         st.success(f"✅ Session saved to history")
580 |     return doc_id
581 | except Exception as e:
582 |     st.warning(f"Could not save to history: {e}")
583 |     return None
584 | ```
585 | 
586 | **User Impact**: Warning message, results still available
587 | 
588 | #### 4. Query Failure
589 | 
590 | ```python
591 | try:
592 |     response = self.client.post_find(...)
593 |     return response.get('docs', [])
594 | except Exception as e:
595 |     print(f"Warning: Failed to retrieve history: {e}")
596 |     return []
597 | ```
598 | 
599 | **User Impact**: Empty history shown, no crash
600 | 
601 | ---
602 | 
603 | ## Performance Considerations
604 | 
605 | ### Document Size
606 | 
607 | - **Typical session**: 5-50 KB
608 | - **Large session** (100+ tables): 100-500 KB
609 | - **Recommendation**: Monitor document sizes, consider compression for very large schemas
610 | 
611 | ### Query Performance
612 | 
613 | - **Indexed fields**: `type`, `user_id`, `timestamp`
614 | - **Query limit**: Default 10 sessions per user
615 | - **Recommendation**: Create indexes for frequently queried fields
616 | 
617 | ### Rate Limits
618 | 
619 | **Lite Plan**:
620 | - 20 lookups/second
621 | - 10 writes/second
622 | - 1 GB storage
623 | 
624 | **Standard Plan**:
625 | - Provisioned capacity units
626 | - Scalable based on needs
627 | 
628 | ### Optimization Tips
629 | 
630 | 1. **Batch Operations**: Group multiple saves if processing multiple files
631 | 2. **Selective Fields**: Only store necessary data
632 | 3. **Compression**: Consider compressing large transformation logs
633 | 4. **Pagination**: Implement pagination for history views
634 | 5. **Caching**: Cache frequently accessed sessions
635 | 
636 | ---
637 | 
638 | ## Testing
639 | 
640 | ### Unit Tests
641 | 
642 | Location: `tests/test_cloudant_integration.py`
643 | 
644 | **Test Coverage**:
645 | - Configuration validation
646 | - Client initialization
647 | - Session saving
648 | - History retrieval
649 | - Statistics calculation
650 | - Error handling
651 | - Graceful degradation
652 | 
653 | **Run Tests**:
654 | ```bash
655 | # All tests
656 | pytest tests/test_cloudant_integration.py -v
657 | 
658 | # Specific test
659 | pytest tests/test_cloudant_integration.py::test_save_modernization_session -v
660 | 
661 | # With coverage
662 | pytest tests/test_cloudant_integration.py --cov=storage --cov=config
663 | ```
664 | 
665 | ### Integration Testing
666 | 
667 | **Manual Test Checklist**:
668 | 
669 | 1. ✅ Upload SQL file without Cloudant configured
670 | 2. ✅ Configure Cloudant and restart app
671 | 3. ✅ Upload SQL file and verify session saved
672 | 4. ✅ Check Cloudant dashboard for document
673 | 5. ✅ View history in app (if UI implemented)
674 | 6. ✅ Test with invalid credentials
675 | 7. ✅ Test with network disconnected
676 | 8. ✅ Test with large SQL file (>1MB)
677 | 
678 | ---
679 | 
680 | ## Development Guidelines
681 | 
682 | ### Adding New Fields to Session Document
683 | 
684 | 1. **Update Schema** in `save_modernization_session()`:
685 |    ```python
686 |    document = {
687 |        # ... existing fields ...
688 |        'new_field': new_value
689 |    }
690 |    ```
691 | 
692 | 2. **Update Tests**:
693 |    ```python
694 |    def test_new_field_saved():
695 |        # Test that new field is saved correctly
696 |        pass
697 |    ```
698 | 
699 | 3. **Update Documentation**: Add field to schema section
700 | 
701 | ### Adding New Query Methods
702 | 
703 | 1. **Add Method** to `CloudantClient`:
704 |    ```python
705 |    def get_sessions_by_criteria(self, criteria: Dict) -> List[Dict]:
706 |        """Get sessions matching criteria"""
707 |        if not self.client:
708 |            return []
709 |        
710 |        try:
711 |            selector = {'type': 'modernization_session', **criteria}
712 |            response = self.client.post_find(
713 |                db=self.database_name,
714 |                selector=selector
715 |            ).get_result()
716 |            return response.get('docs', [])
717 |        except Exception as e:
718 |            print(f"Warning: Query failed: {e}")
719 |            return []
720 |    ```
721 | 
722 | 2. **Add Tests**:
723 |    ```python
724 |    def test_get_sessions_by_criteria():
725 |        # Test new query method
726 |        pass
727 |    ```
728 | 
729 | 3. **Document API**: Add to API Reference section
730 | 
731 | ### Best Practices
732 | 
733 | 1. **Always check `if not self.client`** before operations
734 | 2. **Use try-except** for all Cloudant operations
735 | 3. **Return sensible defaults** on errors (None, [], {})
736 | 4. **Log warnings** but don't crash
737 | 5. **Test graceful degradation** scenarios
738 | 6. **Document all public methods**
739 | 7. **Keep documents under 1MB** when possible
740 | 8. **Use ISO 8601 UTC** for all timestamps
741 | 
742 | ---
743 | 
744 | ## Troubleshooting
745 | 
746 | ### Debug Mode
747 | 
748 | Enable debug logging:
749 | ```python
750 | import logging
751 | logging.basicConfig(level=logging.DEBUG)
752 | ```
753 | 
754 | ### Common Issues
755 | 
756 | 1. **"Database not found"**: Auto-creation failed, create manually
757 | 2. **"Authentication failed"**: Check API key and URL
758 | 3. **"Rate limit exceeded"**: Upgrade plan or reduce frequency
759 | 4. **"Document too large"**: Reduce transformation log detail
760 | 
761 | ### Monitoring
762 | 
763 | Check these metrics:
764 | - Document count in database
765 | - Average document size
766 | - Query response times
767 | - Error rates in logs
768 | - Storage usage in IBM Cloud dashboard
769 | 
770 | ---
771 | 
772 | ## References
773 | 
774 | - [IBM Cloudant Documentation](https://cloud.ibm.com/docs/Cloudant)
775 | - [Cloudant Python SDK](https://github.com/IBM/cloudant-python-sdk)
776 | - [Cloudant Query (Mango)](https://cloud.ibm.com/docs/Cloudant?topic=Cloudant-query)
777 | - [IBM Cloud IAM](https://cloud.ibm.com/docs/account?topic=account-iamoverview)
778 | 
779 | ---
780 | 
781 | **Made with IBM Bob** 🤖
</content>
</file>
<file><path>docs/CLOUDANT_INTEGRATION_PLAN.md</path>
<content lines="1-934">
  1 | # IBM Cloudant NoSQL Integration Plan for LegacyLink AI
  2 | 
  3 | ## Overview
  4 | 
  5 | This document outlines the step-by-step plan to integrate IBM Cloudant NoSQL database to save and retrieve users' modernization history in the LegacyLink AI project.
  6 | 
  7 | ## Why IBM Cloudant?
  8 | 
  9 | IBM Cloudant is a fully managed, distributed NoSQL database optimized for heavy workloads and fast-growing web and mobile applications. It's ideal for this project because:
 10 | 
 11 | - **Serverless**: No infrastructure management required
 12 | - **Scalable**: Handles growing user data automatically
 13 | - **JSON-based**: Perfect for storing modernization metadata
 14 | - **IBM Integration**: Aligns with IBM Bob Hackathon theme
 15 | - **Global availability**: Built on Apache CouchDB with multi-region support
 16 | 
 17 | ---
 18 | 
 19 | ## Architecture Overview
 20 | 
 21 | ```mermaid
 22 | graph TD
 23 |     A[User uploads SQL file] --> B[Process SQL file]
 24 |     B --> C[Generate ORM models]
 25 |     C --> D[Create ZIP package]
 26 |     D --> E[Save to Cloudant]
 27 |     E --> F[Return ZIP to user]
 28 |     
 29 |     G[User requests history] --> H[Query Cloudant]
 30 |     H --> I[Display history in UI]
 31 |     
 32 |     E --> J[Cloudant Database]
 33 |     H --> J
 34 | ```
 35 | 
 36 | ---
 37 | 
 38 | ## Data Schema Design
 39 | 
 40 | ### Document Structure in Cloudant
 41 | 
 42 | Each modernization session will be stored as a JSON document:
 43 | 
 44 | ```json
 45 | {
 46 |   "_id": "session_2026-05-16T14:35:22_abc123",
 47 |   "_rev": "1-xyz",
 48 |   "type": "modernization_session",
 49 |   "user_id": "anonymous_user_xyz",
 50 |   "session_id": "abc123",
 51 |   "timestamp": "2026-05-16T14:35:22.293Z",
 52 |   "input": {
 53 |     "filename": "legacy_customer_schema.sql",
 54 |     "file_size": 2048,
 55 |     "upload_timestamp": "2026-05-16T14:35:22.293Z"
 56 |   },
 57 |   "processing": {
 58 |     "table_count": 3,
 59 |     "column_count": 25,
 60 |     "transformation_count": 42,
 61 |     "processing_time_ms": 1250
 62 |   },
 63 |   "output": {
 64 |     "tables": [
 65 |       {
 66 |         "original_name": "tbl_CUST_MSTR_2012_v2",
 67 |         "clean_name": "Customer",
 68 |         "table_name": "customers",
 69 |         "column_count": 8
 70 |       }
 71 |     ],
 72 |     "generated_files": [
 73 |       "models.py",
 74 |       "database.py",
 75 |       "test_models.py",
 76 |       "README.md",
 77 |       "modernization_report.md",
 78 |       "requirements.txt"
 79 |     ]
 80 |   },
 81 |   "transformations": [
 82 |     {
 83 |       "type": "table_name",
 84 |       "original": "tbl_CUST_MSTR_2012_v2",
 85 |       "normalized": "Customer",
 86 |       "rules_applied": ["remove_prefix", "expand_abbreviation", "remove_version"]
 87 |     },
 88 |     {
 89 |       "type": "column_name",
 90 |       "table": "Customer",
 91 |       "original": "vch_fname",
 92 |       "normalized": "first_name",
 93 |       "rules_applied": ["remove_type_prefix", "expand_abbreviation"]
 94 |     }
 95 |   ],
 96 |   "metadata": {
 97 |     "app_version": "1.0.0",
 98 |     "bob_assisted": true,
 99 |     "success": true
100 |   }
101 | }
102 | ```
103 | 
104 | ### Index Design
105 | 
106 | Create indexes for efficient querying:
107 | 
108 | 1. **By User ID**: `user_id` (for retrieving user history)
109 | 2. **By Timestamp**: `timestamp` (for chronological sorting)
110 | 3. **By Session ID**: `session_id` (for unique session lookup)
111 | 4. **By Type**: `type` (for filtering document types)
112 | 
113 | ---
114 | 
115 | ## Implementation Steps
116 | 
117 | ### Step 1: Set Up IBM Cloudant Account
118 | 
119 | **Prerequisites:**
120 | - IBM Cloud account
121 | - Cloudant service instance
122 | 
123 | **Actions:**
124 | 1. Create IBM Cloud account at https://cloud.ibm.com
125 | 2. Create a Cloudant service instance
126 | 3. Generate service credentials with appropriate permissions
127 | 4. Note down the following credentials:
128 |    - `CLOUDANT_URL`
129 |    - `CLOUDANT_APIKEY`
130 |    - `CLOUDANT_DATABASE_NAME`
131 | 
132 | **Security Note:** Never commit credentials to Git. Use environment variables or `.env` file (add to `.gitignore`).
133 | 
134 | ---
135 | 
136 | ### Step 2: Install IBM Cloudant SDK
137 | 
138 | **Update [`requirements.txt`](requirements.txt:1-4):**
139 | 
140 | ```text
141 | streamlit>=1.28.0
142 | pandas>=2.0.0
143 | sqlalchemy>=2.0.0
144 | pytest>=7.0.0
145 | ibmcloudant>=0.7.0
146 | python-dotenv>=1.0.0
147 | ```
148 | 
149 | **Installation command:**
150 | ```bash
151 | pip install ibmcloudant python-dotenv
152 | ```
153 | 
154 | ---
155 | 
156 | ### Step 3: Create Configuration Management
157 | 
158 | **Create new file: `.env.example`**
159 | 
160 | ```env
161 | # IBM Cloudant Configuration
162 | CLOUDANT_URL=https://your-instance.cloudantnosqldb.appdomain.cloud
163 | CLOUDANT_APIKEY=your-api-key-here
164 | CLOUDANT_DATABASE_NAME=legacylink_history
165 | 
166 | # Optional: Enable/disable history tracking
167 | ENABLE_HISTORY_TRACKING=true
168 | ```
169 | 
170 | **Create new file: `config/cloudant_config.py`**
171 | 
172 | ```python
173 | """
174 | Cloudant configuration management for LegacyLink AI
175 | """
176 | import os
177 | from dotenv import load_dotenv
178 | 
179 | # Load environment variables
180 | load_dotenv()
181 | 
182 | class CloudantConfig:
183 |     """Configuration for IBM Cloudant connection"""
184 |     
185 |     def __init__(self):
186 |         self.url = os.getenv('CLOUDANT_URL')
187 |         self.apikey = os.getenv('CLOUDANT_APIKEY')
188 |         self.database_name = os.getenv('CLOUDANT_DATABASE_NAME', 'legacylink_history')
189 |         self.enabled = os.getenv('ENABLE_HISTORY_TRACKING', 'true').lower() == 'true'
190 |     
191 |     def is_configured(self) -> bool:
192 |         """Check if Cloudant is properly configured"""
193 |         return bool(self.url and self.apikey and self.enabled)
194 |     
195 |     def get_connection_params(self) -> dict:
196 |         """Get connection parameters for Cloudant client"""
197 |         return {
198 |             'url': self.url,
199 |             'apikey': self.apikey
200 |         }
201 | ```
202 | 
203 | ---
204 | 
205 | ### Step 4: Create Cloudant Client Module
206 | 
207 | **Create new file: `storage/cloudant_client.py`**
208 | 
209 | ```python
210 | """
211 | IBM Cloudant NoSQL client for LegacyLink AI
212 | Handles saving and retrieving modernization history
213 | """
214 | from typing import Dict, List, Optional
215 | from datetime import datetime
216 | import uuid
217 | from ibmcloudant.cloudant_v1 import CloudantV1, Document
218 | from ibm_cloud_sdk_core.authenticators import IAMAuthenticator
219 | from config.cloudant_config import CloudantConfig
220 | 
221 | 
222 | class CloudantClient:
223 |     """Client for IBM Cloudant NoSQL database operations"""
224 |     
225 |     def __init__(self, config: CloudantConfig):
226 |         """
227 |         Initialize Cloudant client
228 |         
229 |         Args:
230 |             config: CloudantConfig instance with connection details
231 |         """
232 |         self.config = config
233 |         self.client = None
234 |         self.database_name = config.database_name
235 |         
236 |         if config.is_configured():
237 |             self._initialize_client()
238 |     
239 |     def _initialize_client(self):
240 |         """Initialize the Cloudant client with authentication"""
241 |         try:
242 |             authenticator = IAMAuthenticator(self.config.apikey)
243 |             self.client = CloudantV1(authenticator=authenticator)
244 |             self.client.set_service_url(self.config.url)
245 |             
246 |             # Ensure database exists
247 |             self._ensure_database_exists()
248 |             
249 |         except Exception as e:
250 |             print(f"Warning: Failed to initialize Cloudant client: {e}")
251 |             self.client = None
252 |     
253 |     def _ensure_database_exists(self):
254 |         """Create database if it doesn't exist"""
255 |         try:
256 |             self.client.get_database_information(db=self.database_name).get_result()
257 |         except Exception:
258 |             # Database doesn't exist, create it
259 |             try:
260 |                 self.client.put_database(db=self.database_name).get_result()
261 |                 print(f"Created Cloudant database: {self.database_name}")
262 |             except Exception as e:
263 |                 print(f"Warning: Failed to create database: {e}")
264 |     
265 |     def save_modernization_session(
266 |         self,
267 |         user_id: str,
268 |         filename: str,
269 |         file_size: int,
270 |         tables: List[Dict],
271 |         transformation_log: List[Dict],
272 |         processing_stats: Dict
273 |     ) -> Optional[str]:
274 |         """
275 |         Save a modernization session to Cloudant
276 |         
277 |         Args:
278 |             user_id: Unique identifier for the user
279 |             filename: Name of uploaded SQL file
280 |             file_size: Size of uploaded file in bytes
281 |             tables: List of parsed and normalized tables
282 |             transformation_log: List of transformations applied
283 |             processing_stats: Statistics about processing
284 |             
285 |         Returns:
286 |             Document ID if successful, None otherwise
287 |         """
288 |         if not self.client:
289 |             return None
290 |         
291 |         try:
292 |             session_id = str(uuid.uuid4())[:8]
293 |             timestamp = datetime.utcnow().isoformat() + 'Z'
294 |             
295 |             document = {
296 |                 '_id': f"session_{timestamp}_{session_id}",
297 |                 'type': 'modernization_session',
298 |                 'user_id': user_id,
299 |                 'session_id': session_id,
300 |                 'timestamp': timestamp,
301 |                 'input': {
302 |                     'filename': filename,
303 |                     'file_size': file_size,
304 |                     'upload_timestamp': timestamp
305 |                 },
306 |                 'processing': {
307 |                     'table_count': processing_stats.get('table_count', 0),
308 |                     'column_count': processing_stats.get('column_count', 0),
309 |                     'transformation_count': processing_stats.get('transformation_count', 0),
310 |                     'processing_time_ms': processing_stats.get('processing_time_ms', 0)
311 |                 },
312 |                 'output': {
313 |                     'tables': [
314 |                         {
315 |                             'original_name': table.get('original_name'),
316 |                             'clean_name': table.get('clean_name'),
317 |                             'table_name': table.get('table_name'),
318 |                             'column_count': len(table.get('columns', []))
319 |                         }
320 |                         for table in tables
321 |                     ],
322 |                     'generated_files': [
323 |                         'models.py',
324 |                         'database.py',
325 |                         'test_models.py',
326 |                         'README.md',
327 |                         'modernization_report.md',
328 |                         'requirements.txt'
329 |                     ]
330 |                 },
331 |                 'transformations': transformation_log,
332 |                 'metadata': {
333 |                     'app_version': '1.0.0',
334 |                     'bob_assisted': True,
335 |                     'success': True
336 |                 }
337 |             }
338 |             
339 |             response = self.client.post_document(
340 |                 db=self.database_name,
341 |                 document=document
342 |             ).get_result()
343 |             
344 |             return response.get('id')
345 |             
346 |         except Exception as e:
347 |             print(f"Warning: Failed to save session to Cloudant: {e}")
348 |             return None
349 |     
350 |     def get_user_history(
351 |         self,
352 |         user_id: str,
353 |         limit: int = 10
354 |     ) -> List[Dict]:
355 |         """
356 |         Retrieve modernization history for a user
357 |         
358 |         Args:
359 |             user_id: Unique identifier for the user
360 |             limit: Maximum number of sessions to retrieve
361 |             
362 |         Returns:
363 |             List of session documents
364 |         """
365 |         if not self.client:
366 |             return []
367 |         
368 |         try:
369 |             # Query using Cloudant Query (Mango)
370 |             selector = {
371 |                 'type': 'modernization_session',
372 |                 'user_id': user_id
373 |             }
374 |             
375 |             response = self.client.post_find(
376 |                 db=self.database_name,
377 |                 selector=selector,
378 |                 sort=[{'timestamp': 'desc'}],
379 |                 limit=limit
380 |             ).get_result()
381 |             
382 |             return response.get('docs', [])
383 |             
384 |         except Exception as e:
385 |             print(f"Warning: Failed to retrieve history from Cloudant: {e}")
386 |             return []
387 |     
388 |     def get_session_by_id(self, session_id: str) -> Optional[Dict]:
389 |         """
390 |         Retrieve a specific session by ID
391 |         
392 |         Args:
393 |             session_id: Session identifier
394 |             
395 |         Returns:
396 |             Session document if found, None otherwise
397 |         """
398 |         if not self.client:
399 |             return None
400 |         
401 |         try:
402 |             selector = {
403 |                 'type': 'modernization_session',
404 |                 'session_id': session_id
405 |             }
406 |             
407 |             response = self.client.post_find(
408 |                 db=self.database_name,
409 |                 selector=selector,
410 |                 limit=1
411 |             ).get_result()
412 |             
413 |             docs = response.get('docs', [])
414 |             return docs[0] if docs else None
415 |             
416 |         except Exception as e:
417 |             print(f"Warning: Failed to retrieve session from Cloudant: {e}")
418 |             return None
419 |     
420 |     def get_statistics(self) -> Dict:
421 |         """
422 |         Get overall statistics from all sessions
423 |         
424 |         Returns:
425 |             Dictionary with aggregate statistics
426 |         """
427 |         if not self.client:
428 |             return {}
429 |         
430 |         try:
431 |             # Get all modernization sessions
432 |             selector = {
433 |                 'type': 'modernization_session'
434 |             }
435 |             
436 |             response = self.client.post_find(
437 |                 db=self.database_name,
438 |                 selector=selector,
439 |                 limit=1000  # Adjust based on expected volume
440 |             ).get_result()
441 |             
442 |             docs = response.get('docs', [])
443 |             
444 |             total_sessions = len(docs)
445 |             total_tables = sum(doc.get('processing', {}).get('table_count', 0) for doc in docs)
446 |             total_columns = sum(doc.get('processing', {}).get('column_count', 0) for doc in docs)
447 |             total_transformations = sum(doc.get('processing', {}).get('transformation_count', 0) for doc in docs)
448 |             
449 |             return {
450 |                 'total_sessions': total_sessions,
451 |                 'total_tables_processed': total_tables,
452 |                 'total_columns_normalized': total_columns,
453 |                 'total_transformations': total_transformations,
454 |                 'unique_users': len(set(doc.get('user_id') for doc in docs))
455 |             }
456 |             
457 |         except Exception as e:
458 |             print(f"Warning: Failed to retrieve statistics from Cloudant: {e}")
459 |             return {}
460 | ```
461 | 
462 | **Create new file: `storage/__init__.py`**
463 | 
464 | ```python
465 | """Storage module for LegacyLink AI"""
466 | from .cloudant_client import CloudantClient
467 | 
468 | __all__ = ['CloudantClient']
469 | ```

472 | 
473 | ### Step 5: Integrate with Main Application
474 | 
475 | **Update [`app.py`](app.py:27-86) to include Cloudant integration:**
476 | 
477 | Add imports at the top:
478 | ```python

481 | from storage.cloudant_client import CloudantClient

488 | cloudant_client = CloudantClient(cloudant_config) if cloudant_config.is_configured() else None
489 | ```

491 | Modify the `process_sql_file` function to track processing time and save to Cloudant:
492 | ```python
493 | @st.cache_data(show_spinner=False)
494 | def process_sql_file(sql_content: str, filename: str = "unknown.sql", file_size: int = 0) -> dict | None:
495 |     """Parse SQL and generate all output artefacts. Cached by content hash."""
496 |     start_time = time.time()
497 |     
498 |     try:
499 |         # ... existing parsing code ...
500 |         
501 |         # Calculate processing time

503 |         
504 |         # Prepare result

507 |             "files": files,
508 |             "zip_data": zip_data,

511 |             "transformation_count": len(tlog),
512 |             "transformation_log": tlog,
513 |             "processing_time_ms": processing_time_ms,
514 |             "filename": filename,
515 |             "file_size": file_size
516 |         }
517 |         
518 |         return result
519 |         
520 |     except Exception as e:
521 |         st.error(f"Error processing SQL file: {e}")
522 |         return None
523 | ```
524 | 
525 | Add function to save session to Cloudant:
526 | ```python
527 | def save_to_cloudant(result: dict, user_id: str):
528 |     """Save modernization session to Cloudant"""
529 |     if not cloudant_client:
530 |         return None
531 |     
532 |     try:
533 |         doc_id = cloudant_client.save_modernization_session(
534 |             user_id=user_id,
535 |             filename=result.get('filename', 'unknown.sql'),
536 |             file_size=result.get('file_size', 0),
537 |             tables=result.get('tables', []),
538 |             transformation_log=result.get('transformation_log', []),
539 |             processing_stats={
540 |                 'table_count': result.get('table_count', 0),
541 |                 'column_count': result.get('column_count', 0),
542 |                 'transformation_count': result.get('transformation_count', 0),
543 |                 'processing_time_ms': result.get('processing_time_ms', 0)
544 |             }
545 |         )
546 |         
547 |         if doc_id:
548 |             st.success(f"✅ Session saved to history (ID: {doc_id[:16]}...)")
549 |         
550 |         return doc_id
551 |

553 |         st.warning(f"Could not save to history: {e}")
554 |         return None

559 | ### Step 6: Add User Session Tracking
560 | 
561 | **Add to session state initialization in [`app.py`](app.py:100):**
562 | 
563 | ```python
564 | # Generate or retrieve user ID
565 | if 'user_id' not in st.session_state:
566 |     import hashlib
567 |     import socket
568 |     
569 |     # Create a semi-persistent user ID based on session
570 |     session_info = f"{socket.gethostname()}_{st.session_state.get('session_id', 'default')}"
571 |     st.session_state.user_id = hashlib.md5(session_info.encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
572 | ```

576 | ### Step 7: Add History UI Component
577 |

579 | 
580 | ```python
581 | def display_user_history():

635 | 
636 | ### Step 8: Error Handling and Fallback
637 | 
638 | **Implement graceful degradation:**
639 | 
640 | ```python
641 | class CloudantClientWrapper:
642 |     """Wrapper to handle Cloudant operations with fallback"""
643 |     
644 |     def __init__(self, client: Optional[CloudantClient]):
645 |         self.client = client
646 |         self.enabled = client is not None
647 |     
648 |     def save_session(self, *args, **kwargs):
649 |         """Save session with error handling"""
650 |         if not self.enabled:
651 |             return None
652 |         
653 |         try:
654 |             return self.client.save_modernization_session(*args, **kwargs)
655 |         except Exception as e:
656 |             print(f"Cloudant save failed: {e}")
657 |             return None
658 |     
659 |     def get_history(self, *args, **kwargs):
660 |         """Get history with error handling"""
661 |         if not self.enabled:
662 |             return []
663 |         
664 |         try:
665 |             return self.client.get_user_history(*args, **kwargs)
666 |         except Exception as e:
667 |             print(f"Cloudant query failed: {e}")
668 |             return []
669 | ```
